- Limit: the gate inspects HTML, not the appearance or the tab order; the panel at 32 % and the own view require a real browser (operator's run, spec 018 §7).

### REQ-RPT-026 — The request to the Provider is built by the server from the row; the browser only contributes the question
The request MUST be built on the server with the current ledger row and only these fields, in this order: `title`, `severity`, `status`, `target`, `summary`, `impact`, `remediation`, `repro`, `cvss`, `cwe`, `cve`, `kb`, `evidence.command`, `evidence.artifact`, `corroboration`, `blocker`, plus `evidence.output` only with the switch on. It MUST ignore `messages`, `system`, `findingId`, `summary`, `model` and any other browser field. It MUST NOT send another row, another workspace, the scope, the content of any artifact file (the path is text, it is not opened) or the evidence output with the switch off. The model MUST be the fixed one.
- **WHEN** an «ask» POST with extra fields (`messages`, `system`, `findingId`, `model`, a field value); a row with evidence output and artifact; the switch off and on; and what a fake Provider received is inspected.
- **THEN** only the listed fields travel, in order, from that row; the browser's fields change nothing; the artifact's path is not opened; the output travels only with the switch on; the model is the fixed one.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `chat_context`, `ask_question`, `CHAT_FIELDS`, `CHAT_MODEL`
- Limit: the row travels whole in those fields: a secret stored in `repro`, `kb` or `evidence.command` goes out to the Provider. The Provider is the one in `.gekko-state/env` (REQ-RPT-028), outside this gate.

### REQ-RPT-027 — Every row value reaches the Provider sanitized and inside a single fence; the question goes outside
Each value MUST go through the replacement of control characters by U+FFFD, then through `sanitize_counted` and end up inside a single `wrap_counted` fence; the previous turns MUST come back sanitized inside the same fence. The operator's question MUST go outside the fence. The page MUST show the count of neutralizations and MUST match the markers the Provider received. Before sanitizing, each value (and each previous turn) MUST be trimmed to `CHAT_VALUE_MAX` (16 KiB) and the whole to `CHAT_CONTEXT_MAX` (128 KiB), in the detail, in «ask» and in the rejection alike, and the page MUST announce «valores recortados: N» ("values trimmed: N"). The sanitizer's expressions MUST have every open stretch bounded (`{0,200}`), so that a hostile 1 MB row does not make the work quadratic.
- **WHEN** a row with false delimiters, system tags, role sequences, ESC and bidi in several fields and in a previous turn; what was received is compared with the page; and a hostile 1 MB row (unclosed openings, repeated `Contents of /`, long lists) in the detail and in «ask».
- **THEN** the Provider receives a single fence, with `[STRIPPED-DELIMITER]` markers and without controls; the question stays outside; the page's count equals that of the markers received; the 1 MB row is shown and asked in under 1 s, with the text sent bounded and «valores recortados» announced.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `chat_context`, `CHAT_VALUE_MAX`, `CHAT_CONTEXT_MAX`, `ask_question`; `.claude/tools/sanitize_target_content.py` `sanitize_counted`, `wrap_counted`, `_INJECTION_PATTERNS`
- Limit: the sanitizer is a list of patterns: an injection with a new shape passes; the sanitizer is quadratic with a long run of unclosed openings, which is why the chat trims each value (16 KiB) before sanitizing; the fence warns the model, it does not compel it. What the model says is treated as untrusted (REQ-RPT-031). Spec 018 adds `sanitize_target_content.py` to the boundary.

### REQ-RPT-028 — It only connects to the configured Provider, at a private-network address, and only inside «ask»
`provider_config` MUST read `.gekko-state/env` without executing it (no links, no FIFO, with a size cap), accept only well-formed `GEKKO_LLM_URL` and `GEKKO_PROVIDER_KEY` and reject the rest without showing the key. The Provider MUST be in 127/8, 10/8, 172.16/12, 192.168/16 or 100.64/10; IPv6 MUST be rejected; a name MUST be resolved once, judged by its address and the connection MUST be pinned to that address (the `Host` header keeps the URL's name). A 3xx response MUST be an error without connecting to the destination. No other route —GET, theme, update, status, apply, undo, clear— MUST open a socket or contact the Provider; outside the selftests, only `resolve_host`, `PinnedConnection` and `ask_provider` open or resolve connections (plus `probe` and `http_request`, for loopback use).
- **WHEN** valid environment files, with a link, FIFO, large, with shell syntax and without URL or key; URLs to 8.8.8.8, `[::1]`, 172.32.0.1 and to each allowed range; a Provider that answers 302; a name with two different resolutions; all routes without «ask» with a socket counter; and the module's AST.
- **THEN** the file is interpreted as data and the bad is rejected without the key; out-of-range addresses give 409 without opening a socket; the 302 does not connect to the destination; the name is resolved once and connected to that address; no other route opens a socket; only those three functions open connections and the module's docstring says so.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `provider_config`, `resolve_host`, `provider_address`, `PinnedConnection`, `ask_provider`, `PROVIDER_NETWORKS`
- Limit: the name is resolved with the system resolver: a hostile DNS that points to an allowed private address is not distinguished from a legitimate one. Traffic goes in clear if the URL is `http` (the operator's trusted network). `GEKKO_CRED_ENV` does not apply. Spec 018 does not test the real Provider (operator's run).

### REQ-RPT-029 — One question at a time, with explicit failures and cuts by time, size and absent browser
While a question is in progress any other (of any finding or workspace) and «Vaciar» ("Clear") MUST answer 409 without contacting the Provider, and the other pages MUST keep answering. Each failure (401, 500, 3xx, bad JSON, empty or huge response, drip-fed, hung, closed connection, absent environment, out-of-range address) MUST answer in Spanish, keep the question escaped, not save any turn or write anything and not come back as context of the next one. The caps: connection 5 s, inactivity 120 s, total 240 s, 4 MiB raw and 32 KiB of response. If the browser leaves, or the module updates or stops, the module MUST close the connection to the Provider within 2 s; what was received until then is saved as «detenido» ("stopped") without proposals.
- **WHEN** eleven failures with a fake Provider; two simultaneous questions and a «Vaciar» during a question; the browser closed mid-response; and an update with a hanging question.
- **THEN** each failure gives the status and the text in Spanish, without turns or writes; the simultaneous ones give 409 with a single connection to the Provider and the other pages answer in under 2 s; the connection is closed in 2 s and the partial stays «detenido» without a button; the update does not wait for the question.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `ask_provider`, `read_stream`, `watch_question`, `CHAT_FAILURES`, `chat_failure`, `ask_question`, `Handler._client_gone`
- Limit: «seconds» is measured with a local fake Provider; a real Provider slow up to the cap holds a thread for up to 240 s. A browser that leaves without closing the socket (network down) is detected when the timeout expires.

### REQ-RPT-030 — The Provider's key only travels in the `Authorization` header
`GEKKO_PROVIDER_KEY` MUST be used only in the `Authorization` header of the request to the Provider. It MUST NOT appear in any response, page, header, history, ledger, module environment or `stderr`, not even on 401, 500 or a malformed key. (That the module's environment does not carry it is covered by REQ-RPT-012.)
- **WHEN** a real module (`serve`) with a canary key in `.gekko-state/env`, successful questions, 401 and 500, and a malformed key; the canary is searched in each response, the history, the ledger and `stderr`.
- **THEN** the canary only appears in the `Authorization` received by the fake Provider.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `provider_config`, `ask_provider`
- Limit: the `.gekko-state/env` file stores the key in clear and any process of the same user reads it; the module does not protect it beyond that. A hostile Provider that repeats it in its response would leave it in the history (only seen by whoever already has it).

### REQ-RPT-031 — What the model says is untrusted text: only three fields and only with an acceptable value become a proposal
The response MUST be shown as escaped text (no script, image, event attribute, link, ESC or bidi). Only the blocks `[[PROPUESTA field]]…[[/PROPUESTA]]` that start on a line, name `summary`, `impact` or `remediation` and whose value passes `validate_field` MUST offer the «Aplicar» ("Apply") button; any other (`status`, `corroboration`, `blocker`, evidence, `severity`, `id`, `cvss`, `cwe`, unknown fields, an invisible value, a marker mid-line) MUST remain text. «Ya lo apliqué» ("I already applied it") (an informative link/button) MUST NOT change anything.
- **WHEN** responses with blocks of each field, empty/invisible values, mid-line markers and hostile HTML/ESC/bidi.
- **THEN** only the three fields with an acceptable value carry «Propuesta para» ("Proposal for") and a button; the rest is text; the hostile HTML comes out escaped and the anchors are the former ones.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `PROPOSAL_RE`, `parse_proposals`, `field_accepts`, `proposal_block`, `chat_turn`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `validate_field`
- Limit: an acceptable value can be false or misleading: the operator reads it and decides; the gate does not judge the content.

### REQ-RPT-032 — Applying and undoing are operator actions, bound to the ticket, the proposal and the row seen
`POST /w/<ws>/f/<line>/chat/aplicar` and `/chat/deshacer` MUST go through the same order of denials as the other web routes (Host, peer, method, `Sec-Fetch-Site`, size, ticket) and through `chat_ticket`, bound to workspace, line, row seen, action and proposal, and with a domain of its own (`estado` / `chat`) that separates it from the status ticket: neither of the two MUST be valid on the other's route. Applying MUST write only the saved proposal's field and `updated` through `set_fields` with `expect` of the row seen, MUST ignore `valor`, `campo` and other fields of the request, MUST revalidate the value and MUST answer 409 (proposal available) if the row changed or 422 if the value no longer passes. Undoing MUST restore the previous value and, if the field did not exist, remove the key (`drop`); MUST reject with 409 if the row changed after applying, if it was already undone or if it was never applied. A previous value that `set` would not accept (empty, spaces, number, list) MUST be warned about, applied and not offer undo. Applying and `findings.py set --<field>` with the same value MUST coincide. Applying and adding rows at the same time MUST keep all the changes.
- **WHEN** crossed tickets (also a status one in the chat and a chat one, with a forged `visto`, in `/estado`); requests with `valor`/`campo`; a changed row; undoing in each state; odd previous values; invalid planted proposals; 24 values by both routes; 4 routes × 24 denials; and ten applications with ten simultaneous additions, three rounds.
- **THEN** the ticket of another proposal, of another action or of the other class gives 403; only the proposed field and `updated` change; 409/422 without writing; the two routes coincide in 24/24; each route denies in the right order without writing or contacting; no change is lost and `findings.py list` exits 0.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `apply_proposal`, `undo_proposal`, `chat_ticket`, `post_chat`, `refusal_outcome`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `set_fields`, `set_row`
- Limit: «applied by the chat» is a module mark (REQ-RPT-033): whoever edits the ledger by hand loses it. The fingerprint does not detect two writes that leave the same row (REQ-LED-007).

### REQ-RPT-033 — The report does not know the chat existed; the «vía chat» ("via chat") mark is only on the web
The report page, `render` and the three downloads MUST be byte for byte equal with and without history and MUST NOT mention it. The detail's section MUST show «vía chat · hora» ("via chat · time") next to each field whose current value was applied by the chat, and only that one; the mark MUST disappear after undoing or when another writer changes the field.
- **WHEN** a workspace with history applied, undone and changed by another writer; the report page, `render_md` and `.md`/`.html`/`.imprimir` are compared with and without history.
- **THEN** the bytes are identical and neither «vía chat» nor `.gekko-web` appears; the mark appears only in the current applied fields.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `chat_marks`, `detail_page`, `report_route`
- Limit: the history is not evidence: if the operator needs to record that a text came from a model, they must note it in the report.

### REQ-RPT-034 — The history lives per finding under `.gekko-web/`, in 0600 files that are neither followed nor mixed
The history MUST be stored in `engagements/<ws>/.gekko-web/chat/<sha256(id)>.jsonl` (directories 0700, files 0600, opened with `web_open` without following links, without blocking and with `O_NOCTTY`, like `_read_regular`), one file per finding and workspace, without mixing and persistent after a restart; the index, the list and the report MUST NOT open it. A hard size cap MUST reject the question that does not fit («vacíala» ("empty it")) without contacting the Provider or deleting anything. «Vaciar» MUST delete only that finding's file. An invalid line MUST make the panel say so and refuse asking and applying, while «Vaciar» keeps working. A history file that is a symbolic link MUST NOT be followed, and «Vaciar» MUST remove the link and not its target. `.gitignore` MUST cover `engagements/*/.gekko-web/`. With `O_EXCL` requested by the caller, `web_open` MUST NOT open a file that already exists (`FileExistsError`), instead of falling back to opening it (spec 023a: the gallery writes each image only once).
- **WHEN** two findings of two workspaces with history and a restart; a history above the cap; «Vaciar»; an invalid line; a link file; and `git check-ignore` over a history path.
- **THEN** each finding has its 0600 file and is not mixed; the question that does not fit gives 409 «vacíala» without a connection to the Provider; «Vaciar» deletes only its own; the invalid line is warned about; the link is not followed and its target stays intact; the path is ignored (without a git checkout that case is warned as «skip» and does not count).
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `History`, `history_parts`, `read_history`, `append_event`, `clear_history`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `web_open`, `web_unlink`
- Limit: the history may contain anything the operator asks and the model answers, including data of the finding; it lives in `engagements/` (not published) and its retention is decided by the operator (empty it or `shred`). A hard link or an intermediate component changed between the check and the use by a process of the same UID is not detected.
Limit: the HTTP gate keeps routes/escaping; geometry and clickable area are tested in a browser.

### REQ-RPT-017 — Local Space Grotesk and Michroma in the signed CSS
The module MUST incorporate trusted Space Grotesk WOFF2 and the Michroma subset of the brand kit (capitals, digits and punctuation, only in fixed module text already in capitals, never in ledger values) in the hash-authorized CSS, without new routes or remote fonts. CSP MUST limit fonts to `font-src data:` and MUST keep the restrictions on scripts, connections, styles and ledger reading.
- **WHEN** the CSS is generated and its font URLs and CSP are inspected, and a browser loads the module with CSP active.
- **THEN** there are two embedded WOFF2, each with a wOF2 signature: Space Grotesk (weights 300–700) and Michroma (weight 400, the subset with sha256 `d71f1f9e…00c2`); font-src allows only data:; the style hash covers the complete CSS. In a browser, weights 400/600 load without external requests.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py`, CSS/CSP and selftest REQ-RPT-017. Packaged licenses: `.claude/tools/OFL-SpaceGrotesk.txt` and `.claude/tools/OFL-Michroma.txt`. Each page's CSS measures at most 100 000 bytes (raised to 100 000 on 2026-11-17 for the interface changes the operator asked for, a session decision reported to the operator: the report's A4 sheet and the chat) (case REQ-RPT-054).
- Limit: font-src allows the data: scheme, not an individual binary; the CSS hash pins the emitted font. The selftest tests structure/policy; the effective load requires a browser. A workspace's font is not read and a ledger URL is not allowed as a font.

### REQ-RPT-035 — Comments in the detail: escaped, scriptless and bounded text
The detail MUST have a «Comentarios» ("Comments") section (native target `#comentarios`, without JavaScript) with the finding's current comments, their text escaped like the rest, `POST` forms with a ticket, and a large file rendered in bounded time. Comments MUST NOT enter the report.
- **WHEN** comments with HTML, invisibles and ANSI; a file near the cap (6000 events); detail without comments; a hostile 1 MiB file of `\n` (a million bad lines).
- **THEN** no tag or invisible character comes out of the text; the page answers in under 3 s; without comments it says «Aún no hay comentarios» ("There are no comments yet"); with the hostile file the page weighs under 200 KB, answers in under 10 s and names the first bad lines and the total (1048576), not all of them.

- Limit: a page with thousands of comments is large (no pagination).
- Spec 024: the «Comentarios» section shows each comment's images with the fixed `<img>` form (`capture_item`) and, if the file is missing or changed, the notice with its sha256 (REQ-RPT-070).

### REQ-RPT-036 — Comment: own ticket, one submission writes once and a broken file does not allow writing
`POST /w/<ws>/f/<line>/comentario` MUST follow the order of checks of REQ-RPT-019 (Host, method, `Sec-Fetch-Site`, 413, UTF-8/fields 400, existence 404, linked 409, ticket 403, editable 409). The ticket MUST have its own domain (`comentario-nuevo` for adding, `comentario` bound to id, fingerprint and action for editing/deleting). With the comments file broken or unreadable there is no form and a forged submission MUST be 409. A finding whose id is not UTF-8 encodable MUST NOT give 500 or leave a comment written (REQ-LED-009).
- **WHEN** add, edit, delete; the same form twice; tickets of another domain, comment or action; nonexistent comment; broken file or link; a ledger with id `F-\udc80`.
- **THEN** 303 to `#comentarios`; the double submission writes once; crossed ticket 403; nonexistent 404; broken file 409 without writing.

- Limit: the ticket is anti-CSRF, not a human in the loop.
- Spec 024: same order of checks: text, attach, Add.

### REQ-RPT-037 — Comments change no report
The index, the list, the report and `.md`/`.html`/`.imprimir` MUST be byte-for-byte identical with and without comments and NOT carry their text.
- **WHEN** the same pages before and after commenting.
- **THEN** the bytes are equal.

### REQ-RPT-038 — Editing: `?editar=1` mode without JavaScript, only if the row is editable
The detail MUST offer «Editar» ("Edit") only if the row is editable (same `editable_reason` as the status); `?editar=1` on a non-editable row MUST show the reason and no form. In editing, `resumen`, `impacto`, `remediacion`, `cwe`, `cve`, the vector and the justification are fields of a `POST` form with `visto` and `ficha`.
- **WHEN** GET with and without `editar=1` over editable rows, with a repeated id and with a broken ledger.

### REQ-RPT-039 — `POST …/editar`: same checks and same rules as `findings.py set`
The route MUST apply the order of checks of REQ-RPT-019 with a ticket of domain `editar` bound to workspace, line and fingerprint, write only through `set_fields` with `allowed=EDIT_FIELDS`, accept and reject the same values as `set` (CWE, CVE, empty, invisibles, huge, vectors) and keep the rejected text. Hostile text is stored as it is and shown escaped. A field the operator did not touch MUST NOT be rewritten: what was sent is compared with what the page showed (controls and invisibles are shown as U+FFFD) and with the ledger, so that editing one field leaves the others byte for byte equal even if they carry ANSI, `\r\n`, invisibles or a lone surrogate. The fingerprint is computed over what the page serves: U+FFFD per control, `?` per lone surrogate (the page's UTF-8 encoding) and, in the one-line fields (`cwe`, `cve`), without CR/LF, as the browser sends them.
- **WHEN** web/CLI pairs over copies; status, chat, comment tickets, of another workspace or another line; GET, other methods, foreign `Sec-Fetch-Site`; hostile text in each field; a row with ANSI, `\r\n` and invisibles in `impact`, or with a lone surrogate in `impact` and a trailing `\n` in `cve`, of which only `cwe` is edited.
- **THEN** web and CLI coincide; `impact` keeps the same bytes in the ledger; the foreign ticket is 403 in both directions; nothing hostile comes out unescaped.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `post_edit`, `edit_changes`, `EDIT_FORM_KEYS`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `set_fields`
- Limit: the web never deletes a field (emptying it is 422); the CLI neither.
- UI-r3: the radios of the «Estado del hallazgo» ("Finding status") form MUST show Spanish labels (Hipótesis, Triado, Confirmado, Por validar, Refutado) and send the ledger values; «Guardar estado» MUST go in a flex row with «Cancelar», not full width. The notice «Edición guardada · <short date time>» ("Edit saved · …") MUST carry the class `flash` (CSS animation of ~4 s: it fades and collapses; with `prefers-reduced-motion` it disappears without animation) and show the date as «17 nov. 2026 19:32»; no JS or `style=`. UI-r3 review: the short time names its time zone (« UTC» if the stamp ends in Z, the offset if it carries one); `.flash` clips (`overflow:hidden`) while collapsing; the radios use the interface font; the fixed assistant keeps the log's own scroll with the composer always visible.
- Spec 024: the radio list adds «Remediado»; the radio and the field «¿Qué se volvió a probar y con qué resultado?» ("What was tested again and with what result?") (`.need-retest`) are shown only with `:has()`; the list carries the `st-remediated` badge, the `?status=remediated` filter and the «Nueva prueba» ("New test") detail only if the row has `retest`.

### REQ-RPT-040 — Concurrent editing: a changed row is 409 and a double submission writes once
A save with `visto` different from the current fingerprint MUST be 409 without writing, showing the operator's text next to the current value (also the CVSS vector) with the new fingerprint; the same form submitted twice MUST write once; with no changes, 303 without writing. The form MUST carry the fingerprint of what it showed in each field (`orig_*`, which survives the 409 and «Calcular» ("Calculate")) and the save MUST apply only the fields that differ from that fingerprint: resubmitting after a 409 does not revert what another writer changed in a field the operator did not touch.
- **WHEN** `findings.py set` between opening and saving; the same submission twice; after a 409, the resubmission of the returned form when the other writer changed `impact` (and, in another row, the vector) and the operator only changed `cwe`.
- **THEN** 409 with «Ahora en el ledger» ("Now in the ledger") (also for the vector); a single change in the ledger; after the resubmission the `cwe` is the operator's and the `impact` and the vector are the other writer's.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `post_edit`, `edit_changes`, `seen_digest`, `vector_digest`, `edit_origin`
- Limit: the fingerprint does not distinguish two writes that leave the same row. `orig_*` is a sha256 of what was shown, not the value (the page is not duplicated); the browser sends it and it only decides which fields count as touched: it authorizes nothing (the ticket and the `visto` fingerprint still rule). A field that the operator and another writer changed at the same time is won by the operator's, after seeing «Ahora en el ledger».

### REQ-RPT-041 — CVSS in editing: the server computes, the metrics and the vector do not compete
«Calcular» MUST redraw without writing and with the same ledger. A written vector and touched metrics at the same time MUST be 422; partial metrics, 422; a vector of another version, 422. The severity MUST NOT be written; if the band does not match, the page warns about it. An already stored vector that is not valid (hand-written row) MUST NOT prevent editing another field or be rewritten: only a vector the operator changed is validated.
- **WHEN** written vector, complete radios, both, partial, v3.0; calculate; a row with `cvss_vector: "garbage"` of which only `cwe` is edited, and another edit that writes an invalid vector.
- **THEN** 200/422 depending on the case and the ledger's sha256 does not change on rejections or when calculating; the `cwe` edit saves and leaves `garbage` as it was; the invalid written vector is 422.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `requested_vector`, `cvss_editor`, `cvss_note`, `post_edit`
- Limit: the score shown is the server's, not a verification of the metrics.

### REQ-RPT-042 — Edit and comment writes: a linked workspace is 409 and concurrency loses nothing
Edit and comment MUST answer 409 if the workspace directory is a link, without touching the target; 10 edits, 10 web comments and 10 from the CLI at once MUST all remain, with `list` and `comment list` at 0.
- **WHEN** workspace linked to another; 30 simultaneous writes in three rounds.
- **THEN** 409 and the other workspace intact; everything is there and the file is read whole.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `locate_row`, `post_edit`, `post_comment`
- Limit: a link changed after the check follows the limit of REQ-RPT-008.

### REQ-RPT-045 — Report template and language: one choice over `included`, nothing else
`report_route` MUST load a single snapshot of templates per request and offer the valid ones through native links, first `tecnico`, then `ejecutivo` and then by name; labels are escaped. Technical is the default `tipo` value. If there is no valid Technical, the page warns and shows no preview, form or downloads; it also lists the other rejected templates. All output uses `included(rows)`: parameters do not add statuses or exclude corroborated confirmed ones. An unknown `tipo` or `idioma` on the page goes back to Technical or English.

- **THEN** the pages say «2 incluidos de 3» ("2 included of 3") and show the same ids; the hypothesis does not appear; the selection carries `aria-current`, the labels come out escaped and the unknown falls to Technical. An added template appears after the built-in ones and an invalid one appears as a notice.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_route`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `included`, `load_templates`
- Limit: a non-empty `corroboration` does not prove an independent second signal (REQ-RPT-021). There is no option to exclude a specific confirmed one: to take it out its status is changed.

### REQ-RPT-046 — Technical report: built-in template and AC1 baseline
`tecnico.json` MUST produce the Technical report from `report_model` and `render_report`: cover, fixed notice, counts and numbered sections defined by the file, with the included findings in ledger order. The draft's values are limited to their permitted keys; the cover shows them as text and the sections as escaped text. For the 16 AC1 scenarios (template, language, rich/empty rows and draft yes/no), the Markdown and HTML sha256 MUST stay equal to the 32 literals captured from the engine intact before T5.

- **THEN** the 16 hash pairs match the baseline; cover, counts, sections in their order and included findings appear; no field the template does not allow is incorporated.

- Limit: the draft is the operator's presentation text, not evidence; the AC1 hash covers the case's fixed scenarios, not all possible inputs.

- Spec 024: the HTML sheet and the Technical `.imprimir` carry the block «Remediados (N)» after the open ones with the note, and the count `<li><b>N</b><span>Remediados</span></li>` without a class (`DOC_CSS` does not change).

### REQ-RPT-047 — Executive report: cover, count, table and impact, never evidence
The Executive MUST carry only the cover and the draft's Summary/Scope/Limitations/Recommendations, the count by severity of the included, a table id/title/severity/target and, per finding, title, severity and `impact` from the ledger; it MUST NOT carry `evidence` (command, artifact, output), `repro`, `corroboration`, `summary` or `remediation` in any format (Markdown, HTML, print view).
- **WHEN** rows with canaries in `evidence.command`, `evidence.artifact`, `evidence.output`, `repro`, `corroboration`, `summary` and `remediation`, and a title with `|`, backticks and `## `; the Executive in the three formats and the two languages.
- **THEN** no canary appears; there is one table row and one `### ` per included (four cells per row with the GFM rule) and the hostile title creates no headings; without included it says there are no findings and draws no table.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `report_model`, `render_report`, `_md_finding`, `_md_short`; `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_body_for`, `finding_html`, `short_html`, `report_html`
- Limit: `impact` is free text from the ledger: it can reveal what the operator or the agent wrote there.
- Spec 024: the Executive (Markdown) carries the block «Remediados (N)» after the open ones; it shows the retest note (operator's text, not evidence); a remediated row without a note or without corroboration does not come out and its old `blocker` neither (REQ-LED-011).
- Spec 024: the same in the Executive's HTML, which shows the note (operator's text, not evidence).

### REQ-RPT-048 — Drafts: one file per template and workspace, written only by the module with ticket and fingerprint
Each template's draft MUST live in `engagements/<ws>/.gekko-web/borradores/<name>.json` (regular, `0600`, folder `0700`, opened without following links, covered by `.gitignore`). `POST /w/<ws>/reporte/borrador` MUST apply the defenses of REQ-RPT-019 in their order (Host and user, method and route, `Sec-Fetch-Site`, 413 over `DRAFT_MAX_BODY` —192 KiB: eleven fields of 4096 bytes written as `%XX`—, strict UTF-8, existing and unlinked workspace, HMAC ticket bound to workspace, name and seen sha256, 409 if it changed) and only accept the fields of `plantillas[name].datos["borrador"]` (4096 bytes each; one line in the six one-line ones; no per-finding field). `previsualizar` ("preview") MUST re-render with what was sent without writing; `guardar` ("save") MUST write only the fields with text through a temporary file and `os.replace` under `flock`; saving one type MUST NOT change the other or another workspace.
- **WHEN** save, preview and resubmit with an old fingerprint; absent, false ticket, of another template or workspace; foreign origin; GET, PUT; broken UTF-8; nonexistent or linked workspace; one-line field with a line break, of 4097 bytes; eleven fields of 1365 `€`; a symbolic link planted in `.gekko-web/`, `borradores/` or `<name>.json`; a `flock` held by another process over `borradores/`; a failed `os.replace`; eight saves with the same fingerprint; a 409 and a 422 requested from another tab; status, edit, chat and comment tickets presented to the form.
- **THEN** 303 on save, 200 without writing on preview, 409 with the operator's text kept and the current saved text on an old fingerprint (a second click does not erase what was not seen), the preview, the 409 and the 422 without download links and with `volver` to the report page, 403 without writing on an invalid ticket or origin, 404/405/400/413/422 as the case may be, exactly 409 without touching the other workspace or creating folders on a linked workspace, 409 after a bounded wait (`DRAFT_LOCK_SECONDS`, 3 s) if another process holds the `flock`, one rejection and no orphan temporary if the store changes mid-save, exactly one success among concurrent saves with the same fingerprint, 403 on a ticket of another form, rejection without touching the destination on each link; the maximum draft is stored and reread the same by the web and by `render --borrador`.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `post_draft`, `save_draft`, `read_draft`, `draft_form`, `locate_workspace`, `body_limit`, `DRAFT_MAX_BODY`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `validate_draft`, `web_open`
- Limit: a draft can carry data of the target or the client's real name (`engagement-authorization.md` §2): it stays gitignored and inside the engagement's retention, and the download leaves the workspace. The ticket is CSRF, not a human in the loop (REQ-RPT-019). A link changed between the check and the opening follows the limit of REQ-RPT-008 (if the store changes, the save is rejected with `not_regular` and the temporary is deleted; there is no 500). The `flock` is advisory: whoever does not request it (a root in the container) writes anyway, and whoever holds it only delays the save `DRAFT_LOCK_SECONDS`. The 409 for an old fingerprint shows the current saved text next to the one sent; there is no merge. A hand-edited draft that does not validate is shown as an error and its downloads give 409.
- Spec 024: the 409 of an old save carries the pinned script and the report's CSP (REQ-RPT-071).

### REQ-RPT-049 — `findings.py render --tipo` is the same projection as the web
The web's `.md` MUST be byte for byte `findings.py render --tipo <name> [--borrador F] --lang L` when both use the same template, saved draft, language and workspace. Each page request computes its Provenance and links from a single snapshot of templates; the CLI loads the current templates when invoked. The page MUST show the sha256 of ledger, draft, template, `.md` and `.html`; with a brand it adds the logo's sha256. Each hash identifies the bytes used in that request.
- **WHEN** valid templates, both languages with a draft: the `.md` download, the CLI by subprocess and the page's hashes against the downloaded files.
- **THEN** the `.md` files match the CLI as long as the inputs and hashes match; the Provenance hashes identify the downloaded files and the template used in the request.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_route`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `cmd_render`, `render_report`
- Limit: the CLI reads the draft with cap `DRAFT_MAX_BYTES` and validates it; a draft that the operator hand-edits into something invalid is not projected anywhere.

### REQ-RPT-050 — Downloads bound to the ledger, draft and template version
Each download MUST use a single snapshot of templates and receive `tipo`, `idioma`, `ledger`, `borrador` and `plantilla`. An absent draft equals `ninguno` ("none"); if the ledger, draft or template sha do not match the snapshot, or the draft does not validate, it answers 409 without a file. An absent `plantilla` uses the current version (P1). If the template has a logo, `logo=<sha256>` must match; a different pin answers 409 without a file and an absent parameter uses that snapshot's logo. A non-empty unknown `tipo` in a download answers 404; the page goes back to Technical. With explicit `tipo` the name is `<ws>-reporte-<tipo>-<idioma>.<ext>`; without it, it keeps the name from spec 017.
- **WHEN** a download with current hashes, with any old or malformed hash, without `plantilla`/`logo`, with a changed logo pin, unknown type, and a draft planted as a directory.
- **THEN** 200 with current hashes or absent optional pins; 409 without a file if ledger, draft, template or logo changes; 404 for an unknown type; the compatible link from 017 keeps serving when the fingerprints match; an invalid draft is warned about on the page and the download gives 409.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_route`
- Limit: pinned links stop serving when the file changes; those that omit `plantilla=` or `logo=` use the current snapshot. Draft versions are not kept. A FIFO, directory, link or invalid JSON in `borradores/<name>.json` is warned about on the page and the downloads of that template give 409. The preview, the 409 and the 422 offer no downloads; the Provenance links and sha256 correspond to the saved draft, not to the unsaved text.

### REQ-RPT-051 — Hostile content in ledger, draft and template texts
The values of the ledger, draft and template texts MUST come out escaped in page, preview and HTML; the `.md` keeps them inside spans or fences so that a CommonMark viewer does not interpret an image, link or HTML. A template only admits the charset described in REQ-RPT-055; a validated brand can only produce the logo allowed by REQ-RPT-007/058. No view or download opens a connection.
- **WHEN** the values of `HOSTILE` and ```` ``` ```` with line breaks in each field of the draft and the ledger title with `|`, backticks and `## `, in the two types; pages and downloads under a socket audit.
- **THEN** with spans and fences removed, the `.md` carries no `<`, `![`, `](` or forged headings; no hostile input creates markup. The responses carry no `<script`, events or ESC; `<img>` only appears as the clean data logo of a validated brand preview (REQ-RPT-007). There are no connections.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `validate_draft`, `render_report`, `_fence`, `_code`; `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `draft_form`, `report_body_for`, `finding_html`, `short_html`, `esc`
- Limit: a viewer that does not respect spans or fences could interpret the text (limit of REQ-RPT-022); the draft's text is read literally.
- Spec 024: the hostile report pages carry only the pinned script (`markup_problems(report=True)`); the hostile cases stay green.

### REQ-RPT-052 — Brand and accent in preview and downloads
The `.html` MUST carry a single `data:` URL in its signed CSS: Gekko's horizontal PNG without a brand, or the clean PNG/JPEG bytes of the validated brand (REQ-RPT-058). `print-color-adjust:exact` and the internal CSP admit only that style and `img-src data:`. Without its own color, the accent uses the name from `ACCENTS` and its fixed class from `ACCENT_CLASS`; with a validated color, the downloads can use only `acento-marca` and `.acento-marca{--al:#rrggbb;--an:#rrggbb}`. The page's palette class remains the named one and its CSS/CSP receive neither logo nor template color; no other template value enters classes or CSS. `.imprimir` serves the same bytes as `.html` (REQ-RPT-024). The reports add no date; the workspace only appears in the eyebrow when the route or the CLI passes it.
- **WHEN** the two types, the languages, with and without draft, each accent, and a table of long titles.
- **THEN** the CSS URL matches the corresponding clean logo; the HTML contains no `<img>`, `<a>` tags, scripts or external resources and is deterministic. The preview uses the fixed class of the named accent; the HTML uses that class without its own color and only `acento-marca` with a validated color. Graphite keeps the AC1 bytes; the hashes match their `<style>`, lines meet ≥3:1 and numbers/text over bands ≥4.5:1. There is no date; the workspace only appears in the provided eyebrow.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_route`, `report_html`, `download_css`, `ACCENTS`, `ACCENT_CSS`, `ACCENT_CLASS`, `BRAND_PNG`
- Limit: the viewer must respect the CSP and `print-color-adjust` to see and print the logotype; the gate does not open a viewer (the operator's run does).
- UI-r3: HTML (and its print view) and Markdown carry the prototype's cover (brand on the left, classification on the right, thick rule, eyebrow with the workspace, title, subtitle, 2x2 grid, notice, numbered sections «01 …», count cards Críticas/Altas/Medias/Bajas); when printing, the cover is not split. The downloadable HTML and the sheet share `report_body_for` and `DOC_CSS`; the workspace name appears only in the eyebrow when passed. Cases «UI-r3: the cover…».

### REQ-RPT-053 — Foldable sidebar: `gekko_barra` cookie, 70 px and intact accessible names
`POST /barra` MUST set the cookie `gekko_barra` (`Path=/`, `SameSite=Strict`, `HttpOnly`) only with `plegada` ("folded") or `abierta` ("open"), redirect with `safe_back` and reject a foreign `Sec-Fetch-Site` with 403 without `Set-Cookie`; any other value MUST be ignored without appearing in the response. The `<html>` class MUST come only from an exact cookie value. Folded, the bar MUST measure 70 px with the isotype and each navigation link keep its accessible name; without a cookie it MUST be open from 1280 px and folded from 960 to 1279 px.
- **WHEN** `POST /barra` with each value, hostile values and another origin; `GET`/`PUT /barra`; pages with the cookie at each value and with garbage; valid and hostile `volver`, including the report page.
- **THEN** 303 with the cookie only for the two values; 403, 404 and 405 without a cookie; `class="barra-plegada"` or `barra-abierta` only with the exact value (sharing the attribute with the theme class); the CSS carries 70 px and the width rule; each link has accessible text folded and open; `/w/<ws>/reporte` is a valid destination and `/w/<ws>/reportes`, `/w/../reporte` and `//x` are not.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `post_barra`, `bar_cookie`, `sidebar`, `fold_css`, `BACK_RE`, `safe_back`, `page`
- Limit: the cookie belongs to the host `127.0.0.1`, not the port; it can only be `plegada` or `abierta`. The CSS is not measured in a browser in the gate.

### REQ-RPT-054 — Brand and fonts in the pages: no «G», no external origin and no script
The bar MUST draw the kit's logotype or isotype from the CSS (without «G» or `<img>`), each page's CSS MUST weigh at most 100 000 bytes (with the gallery, the Evidence block and the enlargement: 98 197 measured on 2026-11-18; raised to 100 000 on 2026-11-17 for the interface changes the operator asked for, a session decision reported to the operator: the report's A4 sheet and the chat), Michroma MUST be the kit's fixed subset together with its license and only in fixed text in capitals, and no new response (edit, calculator, comments, draft, preview, bar, 409/422/413, downloads) MUST carry `<script`, event attributes or `localStorage`, nor its CSP `script-src` or `connect-src`.
- **WHEN** the main pages and each new response, including the rejection ones; the served CSS; Michroma's WOFF2 against its sha256 and `OFL-Michroma.txt`.
- **THEN** the bar carries `brand-logo` and `brand-mark` without «G»; no page names an external origin; the CSP does not gain `script-src` or `connect-src`; the subset is the one with sha256 `d71f1f9e…00c2` and the license is packaged; the CSS measures at most 100 000 bytes.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `sidebar`, `CSS`, `CSP`, `MICHROMA_WOFF2`, `BRAND_PNG`; `.claude/tools/OFL-Michroma.txt`
- Limit: the weight drops if Michroma or a brand PNG is removed; the cap is set by the gate. Only the fonts of the texts the CSS assigns them are seen: the rest falls to Space Grotesk.
- Spec 024: the «no script» sweep excepts the three responses of the report page, which carry exactly the script and the CSP of REQ-RPT-071; the page CSS stays ≤ 100 000 bytes.

### REQ-RPT-055 — Templates: strict loading and validation
`load_templates` MUST accept only regular JSON, without links, up to 16 KiB and with a slug name. It must reject duplicate keys and non-standard constants, close the vocabulary and apply caps on text, sections and 16 files, printable charset for line texts, palette and contrast. `marca` ("brand") is the only optional root key: a closed object with `nombre`/`logo` required, `pie` es/en and `color` optional; name and footer are printable lines of 1–120 characters, logo is a slug, color `#RRGGBB` with contrast ≥4.5:1 over white. With a brand, the es/en authors cannot contain «gekko». A logo requires exactly one regular `<slug>.png` or `<slug>.jpg` without a link in `marcas/`, up to 256 KiB and 2048 px per side. Every error rejects the whole template with a Spanish message that names file and key; the others continue. A warning is given if a built-in one is missing or invalid, without replacement; the notice about unconfirmed ones is fixed.
- **WHEN** loading invalid JSON, non-regular paths, names outside slug, exceeded limits, keys or values not allowed and absence of a built-in template.
- **THEN** each invalid template stays out with a Spanish error naming file/key, the others remain, files past the limit are not read and a missing/invalid built-in one produces a notice without an invented fallback.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `validate_template`, `load_templates`
- **Limit:** templates may contain false text; in `os` mode the model can edit them; their sha256 and `git diff` make the change visible.

### REQ-RPT-056 — No template changes the invariants
Templates MUST project only `included(rows)`, never the KB; keep in Markdown and HTML the fixed review notice and the escapes `_code`/`_fence`/`esc`; and use a validated charset for labels and escaped text for lines. A section with only `tabla` ("table") MUST render only that table; `ficha: corta` without `tabla` MUST NOT add a default table. No template text enters a URL, header, `href`/`action` attributes or CSS. The only exceptions are the `tipo` slug and the hexadecimal sha256 values generated by the module in the download parameters; the validated hexadecimal color and the clean logo in the signed download CSS; and the clean `src=data:` of the single `<img class="dmarca">` the module generates in the preview of a validated brand (REQ-RPT-058). No value closes the page `<style>`, is evaluated or opens sockets. The HTTP CSP and the page CSS bytes are identical with brands or additional templates and match `CSS`; `.html` carries an internal CSP with only the hash of its style and `.imprimir` uses that same hash in its HTTP CSP.
- **WHEN** a valid template defines printable line text with hostile HTML content, plus sections, fields and color; the page, its preview, Markdown, `.html` and `.imprimir` are served with included and excluded rows.
- **THEN** only the ids of `included(rows)` appear on the sheet and no KB appears; the hostile values stay as text and do not reach headers, `href`, `action` or `<style>`; all valid templates keep the fixed notice in English and Spanish; the page CSP does not depend on templates and the download hashes match their CSS sheets; there is no evaluation or connection.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` validation/rendering; `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` HTML rendering/CSP
- **Limit:** requesting `evidence.output` in an executive template is valid (REQ-RPT-023).

### REQ-RPT-057 — Adding a format is adding a file
A new valid template MUST appear without code changes after the built-in ones, in load order; Technical remains the default. Downloads, CLI, Provenance and drafts use its slug name and allowed fields; the Provenance shows its sha256 and a different `plantilla=` gives 409. If it has a brand, it also shows the logo's sha256 and pins `logo=<sha256>` in the links; a different pin gives 409 without a file and if it is missing the logo of the current snapshot is used. Drafts live separated by template name.
- **WHEN** a valid template is added or the file whose sha256 was requested changes between page and download.
- **THEN** it is offered and rendered without code changes, with name `<ws>-reporte-<name>-<language>.<ext>`; the sha256 identifies the bytes used and a different version fails with 409.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `load_templates`, `cmd_render`; `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_route`
- **Limit:** a link without the `plantilla=` parameter uses the current version (P1 accepted).
- **Status (2026-11-17, spec 021 T5–T12i):** met. `findings.py render --tipo <name>` accepts each valid template and rejects an invalid name; selector, hashes/pins, downloads, drafts and brand per template have L/WS/W/K cases. Brand templates do not change page CSS/CSP.

### REQ-RPT-058 — Own brand validated per template
A template with `marca` MUST name by slug exactly one `<slug>.png` or `<slug>.jpg` in `report-templates/marcas/`. `load_templates` MUST open that folder with `O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW` and read the file relative to the descriptor, regular and without a link; the logo is PNG/JPEG structurally validated by content, up to 256 KiB and 2048 px per side. `strip_image_metadata` MUST keep only the permitted PNG chunks or JPEG structural segments (it removes auxiliary chunks, text, EXIF, time and private ones; in JPEG it removes APP0–APP15 and COM), without decompressing, and `validate_image` validates the clean copy again. Any failure rejects the whole template. Only clean bytes reach the view or downloads. `report_body_for(..., logo="css")` uses the escaped name as `aria-label`; `logo="img"` produces exactly one clean `<img class="dmarca" src="data:image/png|jpeg;base64,…" alt="…">`, with escaped alt, in the preview. The repeated `<tfoot>` carries classification and name, plus the optional footer, escaped. With a brand, the es/en authors cannot contain «gekko». `color`, if present, is normalized `#RRGGBB` with contrast ≥4.5:1 over white; it only affects `.html`/`.imprimir` through `.acento-marca`. In the signed download CSS only the validated hex and the clean logo appear. The page keeps its CSS/CSP even if the brand is rejected; other templates' brands do not leak. Without `marca`, Gekko's brand is kept; Markdown carries neither logo nor `BRAND_LINE`. Provenance shows the sha256 of the file read; the links pin `logo=<sha256>`, a different pin gives 409 without a file and without a pin the current snapshot is used.
- **WHEN** valid, truncated, altered PNG/JPEG fixtures, of unsupported format/CRC/segments/size/dimensions; 2000 deterministic truncations and flips; metadata with canaries; shapes and keys of `marca`, colors at the threshold, Gekko author; absent, ambiguous, linked, FIFO, directory, oversized, mismatched-extension or corrupt logo; linked `marcas/` folder; loading a correct logo by descriptor; preview, download and print of two brands, a hostile brand and an old pin.
- **THEN** the parser returns dimensions/type or `Refusal`, never an unexpected exception; the cleaning is idempotent, retains the image bytes and removes the metadata; the invalid cases name the file/key and exclude only that template; the file is read once under `dir_fd`; the outputs use only clean bytes, escape name/footer, compute CSP/hash per document and do not leak brands between A/B; the stale pin answers 409 without a file.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `validate_image`, `strip_image_metadata`, `contrast_ratio`, `validate_template`, `load_templates`; `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `report_body_for`, `download_css`, `report_html`, `report_route`
- **Limit:** the validator checks structure, not pixels; arbitrary bytes inside `IDAT` or the JPEG scan data can come out as an inert image. A logo can impersonate another company; `git diff` and the Provenance sha256 make the change visible.
- **Status (2026-11-17, spec 021 T12a–T12i):** validation and web layer implemented; L, WS, W, G and K were green in the main session's run. The mutation that ignores `logo=` made the HTTP stale-pin check fail.

### REQ-RPT-059 — «Adjuntos» gallery: hash-keyed store, append-only index, validation by content and isolation
The gallery MUST validate PNG/JPEG by their bytes with `validate_image` (≤ 5 MiB, side ≤ 16 384, ≤ 50 megapixels according to the header), never by name or `Content-Type`; store each image only once in `engagements/<ws>/evidencia/<sha256>.(png|jpg)` with `web_open` and `O_EXCL` (directory 0700, file 0600) plus an entry in `evidencia/galeria.jsonl`, which only grows; reuse a file that already exists only if it has those same bytes (others: 409 and nothing written); reread and recheck the sha256 (and the validity) of each file on every read; and treat an index with a bad line, over its cap or a name built outside `<64 hex>.(png|jpg)` as a state that blocks writes without opening anything. No GET creates anything and no workspace reads another's.
- **WHEN** a 300 KiB PNG and JPEG with `APP1` are uploaded, the same image again and from two threads, a planted file without an entry with the same bytes and with others; SVG, HTML as PNG, GIF, bytes after `IEND`/`EOI`, broken CRC, 0 bytes, more than 5 MiB, side > 16 384 and 8 000 × 6 251 are sent; a byte of a stored file changes; and the index carries hostile lines or exceeds its cap.
- **THEN** the files remain with the bytes sent, one entry per image with exact keys and `metadatos` ("metadata") only for the JPEG with `APP1`; the repetition changes neither inode, mtime nor entries and the index only grows; the planted file is reused or gives 409; each rejection is `invalid_image` with «archivo N de M» ("file N of M") and «no se guardó nada» ("nothing was saved"); the changed byte, the HTML under its own sha256 and a PNG in an `image/jpeg` entry give `gallery_state`; the hostile lines are bad lines and the rest stays visible.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `prepare_images`, `read_gallery`, `_fold_gallery`, `read_image`, `gallery_upload`, `image_name`
- Limit: the same UID (and the container's root) can fabricate file, entry and reference at once; the bytes inside `IDAT` and the entropy data are not inspected.

### REQ-RPT-060 — Screenshot writes: attach, remove and delete under a single lock
Attaching and removing a finding's images MUST happen in any status, all or nothing, under the ledger's lock and after the rules of `set_row` (fingerprint, unique id, corroboration, blocker): a rejection writes nothing, not even the image. An image MUST NOT corroborate or be mandatory; choosing an image that is not in the gallery, attaching one that is already attached, removing one the row does not have, or choosing and removing the same one MUST be rejected (422); more than 8 per finding MUST be rejected before writing the file (422); a row with an `evidence` that is not an object, or a broken or full index, MUST reject attaching (409) without preventing a status change without images; the index cap MUST be checked before writing any image file (no orphans); deletion from Adjuntos MUST skip, without failing or touching, a row whose `evidence`/`screenshots` has an invalid shape; the multipart admits at most 256 parts (257 is 400). If the ledger save fails, the image and its entry remain and the ledger does not change. A later `findings.py set` MUST keep the row's screenshots. The status form MUST carry a visible «Evidencia» block in any status (outside the status-dependent fields), with «Quitar de este hallazgo» ("Remove from this finding") per attached screenshot and, if Adjuntos is readable, a checkbox per live image that the row does not have and an `input type=file`, without `<script`, event attributes or `style=`; with an `evidence` that is not an object or a broken or full index it warns and does not offer attaching; a rejection keeps the text and the checkboxes, asks to choose the files again and never reflects a hash that is not a live image. The status action MUST accept `multipart/form-data` with a minimal parser (`parse_multipart`): without `boundary`, without closing, with preamble or epilogue, with more than 256 parts, with a file in a text field or text in `archivo`, or with text that is not strict UTF-8 is 400; the `filename` is never used; an old `visto` is 409 before validating the images; a malformed chosen or to-remove hash, or more than 4 files, is 422. The upload (`POST /w/<ws>/galeria`, multipart) and the deletion (`POST /w/<ws>/galeria/<64 hex>.(png|jpg)/borrar`) MUST require their ticket with domain separation (`galeria`; `baja` bound to the hash and the fingerprint: neither the status one nor that of another image opens anything), foreign `Sec-Fetch-Site` 403, and uploading an image that exceeds 200 live ones is 422.
Deleting a gallery image MUST require the current fingerprint (bytes of the ledger and the index) and the confirmation (409/422 if not), reject a file with other bytes («imagen alterada: no se borra» ("image altered: not deleted")), a ledger with an invalid line or an affected row without a unique id, and do it all under the same lock in this order: rows without the reference, removal entry in the index with the findings' ids, `unlink` of the file; a failure at any step MUST leave at most an unused image or a file with its removal entry, never a row reference to a deleted file.
- **WHEN** `set_evidence` in the five statuses choosing, uploading and both; an already attached, absent or malformed hash; removing one that is not there; confirming without corroboration or setting `needs_validation` without a blocker with an image; text `evidence`; broken index; a ninth attachment; a failing `_save`; and `findings.py set` afterwards; the multipart status form with each bad shape, with old `visto`, with malformed hashes and with 5 files; deleting an image attached to two findings, without confirming, with the ticket of another image or of the upload, with an old fingerprint, twice, altered, absent, with a repeated id, racing with attaching it and with an injected failure in `_save`, in the removal entry and in `unlink`.
- **THEN** each record carries `sha256`, `type`, `estado`, `added` and `via`, without repeats; the rejections are `already_attached`, `not_attached`, `conflict`, `not_in_gallery`, `limit`, `evidence_not_object`, `gallery_state`, `no_corroboration` or `no_blocker` with the ledger and the store intact; with `_save` down the image and its entry remain; the CLI keeps the screenshots and confirms without them; the deletion removes the record from the two rows (and only them), adds a removal entry with their ids, removes the file and rejects the rest without writing; the race always ends in (deleted, attach 422) or (attached, delete 409).
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` `set_evidence`, `_store_images`, `gallery_delete`, `delete_stamp`; `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `parse_multipart`, `body_limit`, `post_state`, `post_gallery`, `post_gallery_delete`
- Limit: an upload's body is read whole into memory; a ticket does not prove there is a human.
- Spec 024: the deletion has a prior step (`edit` events of the comments that use the image), the removal entry carries `comentarios` ("comments") only if there were any and the fingerprint adds the size of the comments file when it is not 0 (REQ-RPT-070).

### REQ-RPT-061 — Showing and serving screenshots
The route `GET /w/<ws>/galeria/<64 hex>.(png|jpg)` MUST serve only a live hash of that workspace's index whose file is reread and whose sha256 is recomputed on every request, which validates as an image of its entry's type, with the validated type as `Content-Type`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox`, `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin` and `Cache-Control: no-store`; with `Sec-Fetch-Site` present and different from `same-origin`/`none` it MUST be 403, and anything else (absent or removed hash, another workspace, an HTML with its own sha256, a PNG in an `image/jpeg` entry, a changed file, uppercase, another extension, `galeria.jsonl`, `%2e%2e/…`) 404; HEAD and the other methods 405; the four Adjuntos routes MUST go through the `Host` and user check (REQ-RPT-009). The Adjuntos page MUST reread and recheck the sha256 of each file and show «imagen ausente o alterada» ("image missing or altered") with the registered hash instead of the image; an index with a bad line or over its cap MUST be warned about with the page at 200 and block uploading and deleting (409). The pages' CSP MUST be the previous one with `img-src 'self' data:`; the only `<img>` MUST be the fixed forms of REQ-RPT-007.
- **WHEN** the image is requested with each `Sec-Fetch-Site`, with a foreign `Host` on the four routes, with odd routes, with an HTML named by its sha256 and registered by hand, with a PNG in an `image/jpeg` entry and with a changed byte; and the page with a broken index.
- **THEN** 200 with the exact headers only for `same-origin` and `none`; fixed 403 without workspace name or image bytes for the foreign `Host`; 404 for everything else; the page says so and stays at 200; the uploads give 409.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `gallery_page`, `gallery_image`, `shot_img`, `respond`, `CSP`; `.claude/tools/findings.py` `read_image`, `read_gallery`
- Limit: the cost of rereading and rechecking grows with the gallery (no cache; a one-level cache by `stat` is left for a separate change if a real gallery needs it).
- Spec 024: the comments' `<img>` are the same fixed form and `markup_problems` admits no other.

### REQ-RPT-062 — Screenshots outside the model, the network, the chat and the deliverables
Validating, storing, attaching, removing, reading and deleting images MUST NOT open sockets or processes; the request to the Provider MUST NOT change because the row has screenshots; `findings.py` MUST NOT offer uploading, attaching, removing or deleting images; and `.md`, `.html`, `.imprimir`, `render`, `render --all` and `render --tipo` MUST stay byte for byte equal with and without screenshots on the rows (spec 023a; screenshots in the report belong to 023b).
- **WHEN** the six operations run with `socket.socket` and `subprocess.Popen` patched to fail; a question to the test Provider over a row with and without screenshots; the downloads and `render*` of the same row with and without screenshots.
- **THEN** no operation opens a socket or a process; the request body and each download are identical; no `cmd_*` or `main` names `set_evidence`, `gallery_*`, `prepare_images` or `read_image`.
- Code: `.claude/tools/findings.py` (absence of commands and of network in the gallery functions); `.claude/tools/findings_web.py` `chat_context`, `report_html`
- Limit: proof of absence over the tested paths; a new function that opens a network must bring its own case.

### 3.16 Execution mode: shells on the OS or in the toolbox (TBX)

Cases in `toolbox/gekko-workspace-selftest.ts` and `toolbox/gekko-guard.ts` (marker `req("REQ-TBX-…")`). The mode
decides where the shells of `session_new` are born; `bash`, the guard, the ledger and the report module stay on the host.

### REQ-TBX-001 — The workspace's mode: stored in a file, OS if missing, error if not understood
`parseExecMode` MUST return `os` when there is no file, the mode when the text is exactly `os` or `toolbox` (with spaces or a line break around), and `null` for any other text. The `mode` command MUST print the mode and exit 0, or exit ≠ 0 with a message when the content is not understood. `new` without `--exec` MUST NOT write the file; with `--exec` MUST write it, and MUST reject a value that is neither `os` nor `toolbox`. The launcher and the CLI MUST NOT read a mode file (`exec`) or scope file (`scope.txt`) that is not a regular file (symbolic link, FIFO): they fail with a reason, without staying reading. `writeExecMode` MUST store the mode (spec 014: Pi stores it after an operator confirmation): if `exec` does not exist it creates it exclusively, without following links; if it exists, it opens it without following links, requires a regular file and writes IN PLACE (same inode: the container mounts it by inode, REQ-TBX-007; no rename or temporary file), and MUST refuse a value that is neither `os` nor `toolbox`.
- **WHEN** `parseExecMode` with `null`, `os`, `toolbox`, variants with spaces and line break, and invalid values (`OS`, `docker`, empty, two words); a workspace without a file, with each mode and with invalid content go through `mode`; `new` with and without `--exec`; `exec` and `scope.txt` replaced by a symbolic link and by a FIFO go through `mode`, `check` and `scope`; `writeExecMode` over a temporary directory: without a file, with an existing one (also to a shorter value), with an invalid mode, with a symbolic link (live and dangling) and with a FIFO.
- **THEN** `os` without a file; each valid mode; `null` and exit ≠ 0 for the invalid; `new` leaves or not the file depending on the option; the non-regular files exit ≠ 0 with their reason; `writeExecMode` creates, rewrites keeping the inode number, leaves no remains, and refuses link, FIFO (without blocking) and invalid mode without touching anything.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `parseExecMode`, `readRegularFile`, `writeExecMode`, CLI `mode`, `new`, `check`, `scope`
- Limit: the file is text that anyone with access to the workspace can change (in toolbox, no longer from the container: REQ-TBX-007). The launcher and the CLI do not convert a workspace's mode; since spec 014 Pi can store it with `writeExecMode`, only after an operator confirmation (the caller decides, REQ-TBX-010). The in-place write is not atomic: between writing and truncating a reader sees the new text, never an empty one; if the process dies midway the file keeps one of the two texts or their mix (`toolbox` over `os`), which `parseExecMode` rejects. `readRegularFile` opens with `O_NOFOLLOW` and verifies the descriptor: it covers the CLI, not the guard, which reads `scope.txt` on its own.

### REQ-TBX-002 — When creating a workspace from the menu the mode is asked, and Enter chooses OS
The creation flow MUST ask where the shells are born after the targets and before creating the workspace, MUST accept Enter or `o`/`os` as OS and `t`/`toolbox` as toolbox, MUST ask again on any other answer, and MUST store the choice in the created workspace.
- **WHEN** the flow with injected I/O receives, after the name and the targets: Enter; `t`; an invalid answer followed by `toolbox`; and end of input.
- **THEN** the workspace is created in `os`, in `toolbox`, in `toolbox` after repeating the question, and is not created on end of input.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `createFlow`, `PickIo`
- Limit: function with injected I/O; the real menu in a terminal is tested by `tools/ws-test.sh`, outside the gate. Entering an existing workspace does not ask.

### REQ-TBX-003 — The workspace's container: name, minimal mounts and never adopting a different one
`toolboxRunArgs` MUST produce a `docker run` separated from the process that launches it, with `--init`, `--cap-add=NET_ADMIN`, `--cap-drop=MKNOD` (the container's root cannot create in the workspace a device node that the host would open), `--device=/dev/net/tun`, the name `gekko-tb-<workspace>`, and as only mounts the workspace directory (write) and `.claude/tools` (read-only) at their same absolute paths, the files and the tmpfs of REQ-TBX-007, plus the `.ovpn` read-only if given (`GEKKO_OVPN` MUST be an absolute path). `toolboxPlan` MUST create when it does not exist, start when it is stopped, reuse when it is running, and refuse when it exists with mounts or isolation different from the expected. `ensureToolbox` MUST apply the default ACL to the workspace and to each first-level directory that already exists and belongs to the operator, and MUST return the ID (64 hex) of the validated or newly created container; the ID is used in every later `docker start`/`exec`, the name only in text for the operator. The `toolbox` command MUST exit ≠ 0 with the reason, without creating anything, when docker is missing, the image, the permission or the ACL cannot be applied.
- **WHEN** `toolboxRunArgs` with and without `.ovpn`; `toolboxPlan` for each state with correct and incorrect mounts; the mounts check over test `docker inspect` outputs (exact, `.ovpn` expected/absent/not requested/of different source, with one mount too many, with the whole repo, with the workspace read-only, with another path); `inspect` with `--type container`; the `toolbox` command with an injected docker that simulates: no docker, no image, absent container, stopped, running, with other mounts, and `setfacl` that fails.
- **THEN** the arguments are exactly the expected ones and do not name the repo root or `.gekko-state` or `engagements` except the workspace; the plan is create, start, reuse or refuse as appropriate; each failure exits ≠ 0 with its reason and without `docker run`.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `toolboxName`, `toolboxRunArgs`, `toolboxPlan`, `ensureToolbox`, mounts and isolation check, CLI `toolbox` (prints the ID)
- Limit: the gate's cases use an injected Docker; `tools/toolbox-test.sh` separately verifies real Docker with a temporary root. The container runs as root with `NET_ADMIN`: it is not containment. The ACL lets the operator read, add and delete what the container creates, except reading a file the container leaves in mode 600. A path with `:` cannot be mounted and is rejected. A container created before `--cap-drop=MKNOD` is no longer adopted: the operator deletes it (`docker rm -f gekko-tb-<ws>`) and the next launch creates it again. `tools/toolbox-test.sh` checks with real Docker that `mknod` fails inside the container. Adoption is a check at one instant: it does not prevent the operator (or whoever uses docker like them) from changing the container afterwards, nor a process with docker access from replacing it. Addressing by ID does NOT by itself make a replaced container fail closed: docker also resolves a container whose NAME is the old ID (verified on docker 29.6.1). The guarantee comes from comparing the ID that `docker inspect` returns: the guard's periodic check and `session_new` do so (REQ-TBX-009); operations on shells that already exist (`session_send`, `session_read`, `session_kill`, the panel refresh) do not check it again, and `ours()` only sees that the session is no longer the created one (REQ-TBX-008).

Adoption hardened by T16 and by the later review: before ACL/start/reuse, REQ-TBX-003 MUST require the ID of the configured image and HostConfig with default/bridge network, non-privileged, init, only NET_ADMIN, `CapDrop` exactly `MKNOD`, only /dev/net/tun:rwm, isolated PID/Userns, IPC private, no additional SecurityOpt or DeviceCgroupRules, a single bridge network, rprivate binds, no published ports (`PortBindings` empty, `PublishAllPorts` false), command exactly `sleep infinity` with the image's entrypoint and user, no `ExtraHosts`, `Dns` or `DeviceRequests`, and no restart policy. WHEN each of image, network, privileges, capabilities (also empty or null `CapDrop`), device, PID, ports, command, entrypoint, user, hosts, DNS, devices and restart differs in an injected inspect, THEN it is refused without ACL or start. WHEN the injected docker fails the ACL of a subdirectory, THEN error without `docker run`. Limit: it does not authenticate the Docker daemon nor protect against the operator with Docker access; it does not compare the tmpfs options (size, mode) or the resource limits.

### REQ-TBX-004 — In toolbox mode tmux is invoked inside the container, and without a container it is not invoked
`tmuxCommand` MUST return `tmux <args>` in `os` mode and `docker exec <container> tmux <args>` in `toolbox` mode, without altering the arguments, and MUST fail in `toolbox` mode when there is no container name.
- **WHEN** `tmuxCommand` in each mode with arguments carrying spaces, `;` and quotes; and in `toolbox` mode without a container or with an empty name.
- **THEN** the executable and the arguments are the expected ones, one per element and untouched; without a container it throws.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `tmuxCommand`; `toolbox/shell-session.ts` `tmux`; `toolbox/gekko-guard.ts` shell refresh
- Limit: pure function; the handler harness and `tools/toolbox-test.sh` verify the Docker path. The container name comes from Pi's process environment. Since spec 014 each `tmux()` call of `shell-session.ts` receives the place of the shell it operates on and only without it uses the current mode (REQ-TBX-011). Deliberate exception: the mirror window of a toolbox shell, its mark and its closing (`new-window`, `set-option -w`, `kill-window`) ALWAYS go to the host's tmux, with the place `os` forced, and the attaching client runs inside the container (REQ-TMUX-017).

### REQ-TBX-005 — In toolbox mode the guard blocks the `bash` that names a scope host, except the ledger CLI
In `toolbox` mode, a call to the `bash` tool whose command names an approved scope entry MUST be blocked with a reason that says to use a shell, unless the command is exactly an invocation of the ledger CLI. `ledgerCliOnly` MUST accept only `[python3 ]<path to findings.py> add|set|list|render …` without `;`, `&`, `|`, `$`, backtick, `<`, `>`, backslash or line break. `toolboxBashBlock` MUST decide with the scope the guard already has approved in memory (the file's entries, without the always-admitted local addresses) and MUST NOT reread `scope.txt`: no state of the file on disk (deleted, empty, a line that does not parse, content changed with the mtime preserved) can let through a `bash` that names an approved host. In `os` mode the block MUST NOT apply. The scope, DoS and destructive decisions MUST NOT change with the mode; the handler MUST block with the same reason in OS and toolbox even if the command mentions the container's name.
- **WHEN** `ledgerCliOnly` with valid invocations (relative, with `./`, absolute from the root, with and without `python3`, with quotes in the values) and attempts to get around it (chaining with `;`, `&&`, `|`; substitution with `$(…)` and backtick; redirection; line break; another script with a similar name; another path; unknown subcommand; `selftest`); the real handler with its own guard whose `scope.txt` is deleted, left with a line that does not parse, its content changed preserving the mtime and emptied; the pure function with the approved scope.
- **THEN** only the valid invocations give true; the real handler blocks bash against the target in each of those file states, allows ledger and loopback, keeps scope/DoS and allows session_send and bash in OS.
Spec 014 (AC13): the block follows the current mode from the moment of the change. WHEN the guard's real handler (scope `192.0.2.10`) receives `bash` against that host in `os`, during `levantando` ("coming up") (with the fake launcher CLI in flight), with the toolbox `lista` ("ready"), in `caída` ("down") after a failed start and after `changeToOs()`, and the mode is changed with the REAL `changeToToolbox()` and `changeToOs()` (not by setting the environment by hand), THEN it passes in `os`, is blocked in `levantando`, `lista` and `caída`, and passes again after the return to `os`.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `ledgerCliOnly`; `toolbox/gekko-guard.ts` `toolboxBashBlock`, `loadScope` (`approved`), `changeToToolbox`, `changeToOs`
- Limit: it recognizes hosts like the rest of the broker (IPv4, GAP-12): a target named by domain escapes it. It only looks at the `bash` tool. The exception lets any `findings.py` argument through, including a ledger path outside the workspace. The always-admitted local addresses are the HOST's; in toolbox the VPN lives in the container, so a payload that names the container's own address (its tun0 or eth0) is blocked as out of scope until the operator adds that address to the scope (operator's decision: container addresses are not derived).

### REQ-TBX-006 — The model's prompt says the mode and, in toolbox, where each thing runs
`workspacePromptBlock` with a workspace MUST describe BOTH modes, the same in both (the model does not know which one rules after a live change): in `os` the shells are born on the host; in `toolbox` it MUST name the container (`gekko-tb-<ws>`, also in `os` mode) and say that the shells are born inside, that `bash` and the read tools run on the host and serve for the repo and the KB, that what goes against the target goes through a shell, that the container only sees the workspace (with `exec`, `scope.txt` and `AUTHORIZATION.md` read-only and `.pi-sessions` empty), that an address of the container's own is out of scope until the operator adds it, and how to attach to a shell. It MUST say which mode rules at launch, that the operator can change it with Pi open, that the result of `session_new` indicates where the shell was born (`lugar=os|toolbox`) and that if `session_new` fails naming a toolbox state (`levantando`, `caída`) the model stops and warns the operator, without retrying elsewhere. The `prompt` CLI MUST receive the mode already resolved by the launcher and MUST fail without it: it does not read it again from the file.
- **WHEN** the block for a workspace in each mode (with and without a given container name) and for `none`; the `prompt` CLI with each mode (also contrary to the file's) and without a mode or with an invalid one.
- **THEN** the two texts carry the description of both modes and differ only in the mode they say is in force at launch; that of `none` names no container; the CLI uses the mode received and exits ≠ 0 without a valid mode.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `workspacePromptBlock`, CLI `prompt`
- Limit: it is text for the model: it does not prove the model follows it. It says the mode AT LAUNCH: after a live change the prompt is stale, and that is why the text sends the model to read `lugar=` in the result of `session_new` and not to deduce it.

### REQ-TBX-007 — The container does not change what decides mode, scope and authorization and does not see Pi's session store
The container MUST receive `<ws>/exec`, `<ws>/scope.txt` and —only if it is a regular file— `<ws>/AUTHORIZATION.md` mounted read-only over themselves, and an empty tmpfs on `<ws>/.pi-sessions`. `ensureToolbox` MUST fail without calling docker when `exec` or `scope.txt` are not regular files (lstat, without following links) or when `<ws>/.pi-sessions` is not a directory: it never lets docker create a directory in their place. The launcher MUST create `<ws>/.pi-sessions` on the host before bringing the container up. Adoption MUST require exactly that set of mounts (AUTHORIZATION.md mounted if and only if today it is a regular file).
- **WHEN** `toolboxRunArgs` with and without AUTHORIZATION.md; the mounts check with each piece absent, writable, on another path or of another type; `ensureToolbox` with a container without the tmpfs, with AUTHORIZATION.md mounted or not, and with `exec`, `scope.txt` or `.pi-sessions` absent, links, FIFOs, directories or files; and, with real docker (`tools/toolbox-test.sh`, `toolbox` command against a temporary workspace): a file written on the host in `.pi-sessions` is not seen from the container and one written from the container does not appear on the host; from the container, deleting, overwriting, truncating and replacing by rename each of `exec`, `scope.txt` and `AUTHORIZATION.md` fails and the host copies stay intact; and the workspace by ACL remains writable by the container.
- **THEN** the arguments and the adoption match the expected set; each absence or wrong type exits ≠ 0 with a reason and without `docker run`; in real docker each attempt fails and the host files keep their content and their inode.
- Code: `toolbox/gekko-workspace.ts` `toolboxRunArgs`, `toolboxMountsOk`, `ensureToolbox`; `tools/pi-hitl.sh` (creates `.pi-sessions`)
- Limit: the files are mounted by inode: an edit on the host that replaces the file by rename is not seen by the container until it is recreated (`docker rm -f`); in-place edits are. An `AUTHORIZATION.md` created or deleted after creating the container makes the adoption refuse until it is recreated.
